Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the diff_geom framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 784. The resulting operator achieves bound O(951/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on diff_geom structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 784. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 951. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any diff_geom module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 655. The projective resolution has length at most 470 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to diff_geom theory, we decompose the problem into 655 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 470. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
